1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any precipitation module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 352. The projective resolution has length at most 890 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the precipitation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 352 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 890. QED.
